, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp\,}(\upsilon_{q+1})

\displaystyle\subseteq\bigcup_{\xi,i,j,k,\vec{l},I,\diamond}\mathrm{supp\,}\left(\chi_{i,k,q}\zeta_{q,\diamond,i,k,\xi,\vec{l}}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)\cap B\left(\mathrm{supp\,}\varrho^{I}_{(\xi),\diamond}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)},{3}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\right)\,.

In order to have effective dodging with
\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}
,
q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, we appeal to (
4.13
). Also, using (
5.51
)–(

) and appealing to a similar dodging and upgrading argument which produced the bound (

), we have that
for all
N\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{4}}-2{\mathsf{d}}^{2}-{\mathsf{d}}
and
M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{5}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{supp\,}\psi_{i,{q+\bar{n}}-1}}D^{N}D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{M}\mathcal{H}\left((w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})^{\bullet}\mathrm{div}\left(R_{\ell}^{m}-\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\Gamma_{m}^{3}\pi_{\ell}^{m}\Lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}\left(\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{i+4},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-101}\pi_{q}^{q+\bar{n}}\left(\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{i-5},\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{2}\right)\,,

(5.53)

where we have used (
2.25
), (
3.110
), and (
7.1b
) to conclude the last line. Finally, the estimates in (

) follow from Remark

, and we omit the details now and in the rest of this proof.

In the case
m=q
, we make slight changes in the choices of

,

,

) holds for

. In the cases

, we set
G=\mathrm{div}\left(\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}
,

, and make suitable changes to the parameters and functions based on (
3.109c
) and (
2.38
) for

. Concluding as before, we find that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{supp\,}\psi_{i,{q+\bar{n}}-1}}D^{N}D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{M}\mathcal{H}\left((w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})\cdot\mathrm{div}\left(\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\Gamma_{m}^{3}\pi_{\ell}^{m}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\left(\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{i+4},\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-101}\pi_{q}^{q+\bar{n}}\left(\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{i+4},\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\right)

(5.54)

for all
N\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{4}}-2{\mathsf{d}}^{2}-{\mathsf{d}}
and
M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{5}}
. In the last inequality, we have used (
3.110
), (
2.23
), and (
2.25
) to write that
\pi_{\ell}^{m}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}-1}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\leq 2\pi_{q}^{q+\bar{n}-1}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}-1}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\leq\pi_{q}^{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-150}
.

We can now set

\overline{\phi}_{L}^{q+\bar{n},l}=\mathcal{H}\left((w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})\cdot\mathrm{div}\left(\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}-1}R_{\ell}^{m}-\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}-1}\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}\right)\,,

which is well-defined over various values of
i
since the algorithm used to define
\mathcal{H}
is independent of the value of
i
. Summing the estimate in (
5.53
)–(
5.54
) over the various values of
m
and using Cauchy-Schwarz, (
2.22
), and (
7.13a
) gives (
5.47a
).

Step 2: Estimating the main nonlocal term and remainder terms.
Due to the negligible quality of these error terms, we shall omit most of the details in this portion of the proof. We first finish the application of Lemma
A.12
to the main terms by setting up the nonlocal assumptions and output in Part 3. In the case of
q+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}-1
, we
have that for
N,M\leq 3\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left((w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})^{\bullet}\mathrm{div}(R_{\ell}^{m}-\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id})^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-M}\,.

(5.55a)

In the remaining cases, we have that for
N,M\leq 3\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left((w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})^{\bullet}\mathrm{div}(R_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}\mathrm{Id})^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-M}

(5.55b)

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q+\bar{n}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left((w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})^{\bullet}\mathrm{div}(\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id})^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-M}

(5.55c)

for
q+\bar{n}\leq m<q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
. We will upgrade the material derivatives at the end of
Step 2
.

Next, we must treat the second error identified in
Step 0
, namely the remainder term which includes
e_{q+1}
. This is easily handled using Lemma
A.12
. In the cases
q+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}-1
, we
(
A.51
) gives that

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left(\mathrm{div}\left(R_{\ell}^{m}-\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}e_{q+1}^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{5\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}(\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{2})^{M}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}

(5.56a)

for
N,M\leq 3\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
. Similarly, we have that for
N,M\leq 3\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
and
q+\bar{n}\leq m<q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
,

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left(\mathrm{div}\left(R_{\ell}-\pi_{\ell}\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}e_{q+1}^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}\lesssim\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{5\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{2}\right)

(5.56b)

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q+\bar{n}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left(\mathrm{div}\left(\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}e_{q+1}^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{4\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\right)

(5.56c)

Finally, we must treat the third error identified in
Step 0
, namely the remainder term which includes the differences between mollified and inductive stresses and pressures. This again is a simple application of Lemma
A.12
. In the case of
q\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}-1
, we
have from (
A.51
) that for
N,M\leq 3\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left(\mathrm{div}\left((R_{q}^{m}-R_{\ell}^{m})-(\pi_{q}^{m}-\pi_{\ell}^{m})\mathrm{Id}\right)^{\bullet}w_{q+1}^{\bullet}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{3\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}+1}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}(\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{2})^{M}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}

(5.57a)

In the remaining cases, we have

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left(\mathrm{div}\left((R_{q}^{q}-R_{\ell})-(\pi_{q}^{q}-\pi_{\ell})\mathrm{Id}\right)\cdot w_{q+1}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}+1}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{2}\right)

(5.57b)

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q+\bar{n}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}\left(\mathrm{div}\left((\pi_{q}^{m}-\pi_{\ell}^{m})\mathrm{Id}\right)w_{q+1}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}+1}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\right)

(5.57c)

for
N,M\leq 3\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
and
q+\bar{n}\leq m<q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
.

We can now set

\displaystyle\overline{\phi}_{L}^{q+\bar{n},*}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}^{*}\left[(w_{q+1}-e_{q+1})\cdot\mathrm{div}\left[\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}-1}R_{\ell}^{m}-\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}-1}\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathcal{R}^{*}\left[\left[\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}-1}R_{\ell}^{m}-\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}-1}\pi_{\ell}^{m}\mathrm{Id}\right]\cdot e_{q+1}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathcal{R}^{*}\left[\mathrm{div}\left[\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}-1}(R_{q}^{m}-R_{\ell}^{m})-\sum_{m=q}^{{q+\bar{n}}+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}-1}(\pi_{q}^{m}-\pi_{\ell}^{m})\mathrm{Id}\right]\cdot w_{q+1}\right]\,.

We must now upgrade the material derivatives in the estimates (
5.55a
), (
5.56a
), (
5.57a
) in order to match the bound in (
5.47b
). Specifically, we apply Lemma
A.2
with
p=\infty
,
N_{x}=N_{t}=\infty
,
N_{*}=\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}{{4}}
,
\Omega=\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}
,
v=\widehat{u}_{m-1}
,
w=\widehat{u}_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}-\widehat{u}_{m-1}
, and parameter choices according to (
2.36
), which verifies (
A.4
), parameter choices according to (
2.38
), which verifies (
A.1
), and parameter choices according to (
5.55
)–(
5.57
), which verify (
A.2
). We however emphasize the choice of
\mathcal{C}_{f}=\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}+1}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}
, which can be used to absorb lossy material derivative estimates. We then have from (
A.5
) that (
5.47b
) holds, concluding the proof.
∎

5.4 Stress current error

From subsection
5.1
, the stress current error is given by

-\overline{\phi}_{R}=(\widehat{u}_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q})\overline{\kappa}_{q+1}+\left(\overline{R}_{q+1}-(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})\mathrm{Id}\right)(\widehat{u}_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q})\,.

Next, we recall from (
2.9
) that
\widehat{u}_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q}=\widehat{w}_{q+1}
, and that from (
5.3a
),

\overline{\kappa}_{q+1}=\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}(-\pi_{q}\mathrm{Id}+\pi_{q}^{q}\mathrm{Id}+\overline{R}_{q+1})=-\frac{3}{2}(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})+\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}\overline{R}_{q+1}\,.

Lemma 5.4

(
Definition and basic estimates).

The current error
\overline{\phi}_{R}
satisfies the following.

(i)

We have the decomposition

\overline{\phi}_{R}=\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}+\sum_{m=q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}}\overline{\phi}_{R}^{m,*}\,.

(5.58)

(ii)

For all
N,M
such that
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}{{4}}
and
q+1\leq m\leq{q+\bar{n}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+1}^{-99}\left(\pi_{q}^{q+1}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+20}\tau_{q}^{-1},\Gamma_{q}^{10}\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\right)

(5.59a)

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\overline{\phi}_{R}^{m,*}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{2}\Lambda_{m}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{m-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\right)\,.

(5.59b)

(iii)

The local part
\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}
has the support property,

\displaystyle B\left(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q},\lambda_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+1}\right)\cap\mathrm{supp\,}\overline{\phi}^{q+1,l}_{O}

\displaystyle=\emptyset\,.

(5.60)

Proof.

Recalling (
2.10
), (
2.28
), (
3.77
), and (
3.78
), we define

\displaystyle-\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}

\displaystyle=\widehat{w}_{q+1}\left[-\frac{3}{2}(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})+\sum_{m=q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}\overline{R}_{q+1}^{m,l}\right]+\left[-(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})\mathrm{Id}+\sum_{m=q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\overline{R}_{q+1}^{m,l}\right]\widehat{w}_{q+1}

\displaystyle=\widehat{w}_{q+1}\left[-\frac{3}{2}(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})+\sum_{m=q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}\left(R_{q}^{m,l}+S_{q+1}^{m,l}\right)\right]+\left[-(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})\mathrm{Id}+\sum_{m=q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}R_{q}^{m,l}+S_{q+1}^{m,l}\right]\widehat{w}_{q+1}

(5.61)

\displaystyle-\overline{\phi}_{R}^{m,*}

\displaystyle=\widehat{w}_{q+1}\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}\overline{R}_{q+1}^{m,*}+\overline{R}_{q+1}^{m,*}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\qquad\textnormal{ for $q+1\leq m\leq{q+\bar{n}}$}\,.

(5.62)

In order to prove (
5.59a
) for
\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}
, it suffices to prove the estimate for the second term from the second line of (
5.61
), as it is clear that the first term will obey identical estimates. We first consider the term with the stresses, before handling the term with the pressures next. The crucial first step is to employ
dodging
to eliminate most of the terms from (
5.61
). Specifically, we have from (
2.10
) (which gives that
R_{q}^{{q+\bar{n}},l}\equiv 0
) and (
2.34
) that

\left(\sum_{m=q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}R_{q}^{m,l}\right)\widehat{w}_{q+1}=R_{q}^{q+1,l}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\,.

Therefore, we have from (
2.26a
), (
2.28
) and (
2.26c
) that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}R_{q}^{q+1,l}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{N_{1}=0}^{N}\sum_{M_{1}=0}^{M}\sum_{i^{\prime}=i-1}^{i+1}\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N_{1}}D_{t,q}^{M_{1}}R_{q+1}^{q+1,l}\right|\left|\psi_{i^{\prime},q}D^{N-N_{1}}D_{t,q}^{M-M_{1}}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\pi_{q}^{q+1}(\pi_{q}^{q+1})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q-\bar{n}+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+20}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{10}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+1}^{-101}\left(\pi_{q}^{q+1}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+20}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{10}\right)\,,

where we have used (
7.1c
) to achieve the final inequality.

In order to prove a similar estimate for the term with pressures, we appeal to (
2.11
) and (
2.35a
) to write that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\left[(\pi-\pi_{q}^{q})\widehat{w}_{q+1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\left[\left(\sum_{k=q+1}^{\infty}\pi_{q}^{k}\right)\widehat{w}_{q+1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q}\Gamma_{q+1}^{-100}(\pi_{q}^{q+1})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+20}\tau_{q}^{-1},\Gamma_{q}^{10}\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\right)\,.

Combined with the previous estimate, this concludes the proof of (
5.59a
) for terms from (
5.61
) which involve stresses
R_{q}^{m,l}
and pressure.

In order to prove (
5.59a
) for terms from (
5.61
) which involve stresses
S_{q+1}^{m,l}
defined in (
3.77
), we again employ the dodging results from (
3.83b
) to write that

\sum_{m=q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}S_{q+1}^{m,l}\widehat{w}_{q+1}=S_{q+1}^{q+1,l}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\,.

Then from (
3.86
) and (
2.26c
), we have that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}S_{q+1}^{q+1,l}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{N_{1}=0}^{N}\sum_{M_{1}=0}^{M}\sum_{i^{\prime}=i-1}^{i+1}\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N_{1}}D_{t,q}^{M_{1}}S_{q+1}^{q+1,l}\right|\left|\psi_{i^{\prime},q}D^{N-N_{1}}D_{t,q}^{M-M_{1}}\widehat{w}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q+1}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{q+1}(\pi_{q}^{q+1})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q-\bar{n}+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+20}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{10}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+1}^{-101}\left(\pi_{q}^{q+1}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+20}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{10}\right)\,,

concluding the proof of (
5.59a
).

Lastly, the nonlocal estimate (
5.59b
) follows immediately from (
2.26c
), (
2.28
), (
3.88
), and immediate computation. We omit further details. Also, the support property (
5.60
) can be easily obtained from the definition (
5.61
) of
\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}
and Hypothesis
2.5
.
∎

5.5 Divergence correctors

Lemma 5.5

(
Divergence corrector current error and the associated pressure increment).

There exist current errors
\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m}=\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m,l}+\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m,*}
and pressure increments
\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m}}=\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m}}^{+}-\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m}}^{-}
for
m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1,\dots,q+\bar{n}
such that the following hold.

(i)

We have the equality

\mathrm{div}\left(\frac{1}{2}|w_{q+1}^{(c)}|^{2}w_{q+1}^{(p)}+(w_{q+1}^{(c)}\cdot w_{q+1}^{(p)})w_{q+1}^{(p)}+\frac{1}{2}|w_{q+1}|^{2}w_{q+1}^{(c)}\right)=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\mathrm{div}\phi_{C}^{m}\,.

(ii)

For all
N,M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\overline{\phi}^{m,l}_{C}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{+}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}\left(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{q}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+15},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(5.63a)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\overline{\phi}^{q+\bar{n}}_{C}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{q+\bar{n},l}_{C}}^{+}+\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}\left(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+15},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)\,,

(5.63b)

where the first estimate holds for
m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1,\dots,q+\bar{n}-1
, and
\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}
is defined as in Lemma
3.19
in the second estimate. In addition, for all
m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1,\dots,q+\bar{n}
and
N,M\leq{\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{+}+\delta^{2}_{q+3\bar{n}}\right)\left(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{q}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+16},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(5.63c)

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{+}\right\|_{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle<\delta_{m+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{m}^{-9}\left(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{q}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+16},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(5.63d)

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{+}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{m}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-9}\left(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{q}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+16},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(5.63e)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{-}\right|

\displaystyle<\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\left(\lambda_{q+\lfloor\bar{n}/2\rfloor}\Gamma_{q}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+16},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(5.63f)

Finally, we have that for all
m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1,\dots,{q+\bar{n}}
,

\displaystyle B\left(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+1}\right)\cap\mathrm{supp\,}\overline{\phi}^{m,l}_{C}

\displaystyle=\emptyset\qquad\forall q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq m-1

(5.64a)

\displaystyle B\left(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+1}\right)\cap\mathrm{supp\,}(\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{+})

\displaystyle=\emptyset\qquad\forall q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq m-1

(5.64b)

\displaystyle B\left(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q+1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{2}\right)\cap\mathrm{supp\,}(\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}^{-})

\displaystyle=\emptyset\qquad\forall q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\,.

(5.64c)

(iii)

For all
m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1,\dots,q+\bar{n}
and
N,M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, the non-local part
\bar{\phi}^{m,*}_{C}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\bar{\phi}^{m,*}_{C}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\lambda_{m}^{N}\tau_{q}^{-M}\,,

(5.65)

Lemma 5.6

(
Pressure current).

For every
m^{\prime}\in\{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1,\dots,q+\bar{n}\}
, there exists a current error

\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}
associated to the pressure increment
\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}
in the sense of

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}

\displaystyle=D_{t,q}\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}D_{t,q}\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}(t,x^{\prime})\,dx^{\prime}\,.

(5.66)

The current error
\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}
has a decomposition

\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}=\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}^{*}+\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{{m^{\prime}}}\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}^{m}=\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}^{*}+\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{{m^{\prime}}}\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}^{m,l}+\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}^{m,*}\,,

where the local parts
\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}^{m,l}
and the nonlocal parts
\phi_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}^{m,*}
and
\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}^{*}
satisfy the following properties.

(i)

For
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq m^{\prime}
and
N,M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}^{m,l}\right|<\Gamma_{m}^{-100}\left(\pi_{q}^{m}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}^{2})^{M}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+17},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(5.67a)

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}^{m,*}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\phi_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}^{*}+\right\|_{\infty}<\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}^{2})^{N}\tau_{q}^{-M}\,.

(5.67b)

(ii)

For all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq m^{\prime}
and all
q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq m-1
,

\displaystyle B\left(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+1}\right)\cap\mathrm{supp\,}\phi^{m,l}_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}=\emptyset\,.

(5.68)

(iii)

For
M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, the mean part
\langle D_{t,q}\sigma_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}\rangle
satisfies

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d^{M}}{dt^{M}}\langle D_{t,q}\sigma_{{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m^{\prime}}}}\rangle\right|\leq(\max(1,T))^{-1}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)\,.

(5.69)

Proof of Lemma
5.5
–
5.6
.

Step 1: Analyze the error
. We first decompose

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}|w_{q+1}^{(c)}|^{2}w_{q+1}^{(p)}

\displaystyle+(w_{q+1}^{(c)}\cdot w_{q+1}^{(p)})w_{q+1}^{(p)}+\frac{1}{2}|w_{q+1}|^{2}w_{q+1}^{(c)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}|w_{q+1}^{(c)}|^{2}w_{q+1}^{(p)}+\frac{1}{2}w_{q+1}^{(c)}|w_{q+1}^{(c)}|^{2}+\left(w_{q+1}^{(c)}\cdot w_{q+1}^{(p)}\right)w_{q+1}^{(c)}

(5.70)

\displaystyle\quad+(w_{q+1}^{(c)}\cdot w_{q+1}^{(p)})w_{q+1}^{(p)}+\frac{1}{2}|w_{q+1}^{(p)}|^{2}w_{q+1}^{(c)}\,.

(5.71)

The first set (
5.70
) of terms is simpler because each term has two divergence correctors and thus will be absorbed directly into
\overline{\phi}^{q+\bar{n},l}_{C}
. The second set (
5.71
) is more delicate, so we now rewrite this term using a few algebraic identities similar to the divergence corrector error terms in the Euler-Reynolds system (cf.
[
25
, Lemma 8.10]
).

Taking the divergence operator to the first term in (
5.71
) and using
\mathbb{U}_{{(\xi)},\diamond}^{I,s}
to denote the
s
component of the vector field
\mathbb{U}_{{(\xi)},\diamond}^{I}
(the potential for
\mathbb{W}_{{(\xi)},\diamond}^{I,s}
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}\left(w_{q+1,\diamond}^{(p)}(w_{q+1,\diamond}^{(c)}\cdot w_{q+1,\diamond}^{(p)})\right)

\displaystyle=\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}\partial_{m}\biggl(\xi^{\theta}A_{\theta}^{n}a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{2}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}\right)^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\epsilon_{npr}\partial_{p}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I,s}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\biggr)

\displaystyle=:(\mathbf{C}_{0})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}\left((\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2}\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I,s}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\,.

(5.72)

As we see in the second and the third line, for the time being we omit the summation over the indices
\diamond,i,j,k,\xi,\vec{l},I
for convenience, until we reintroduce them. In the first equality, observe that we have commuted
\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}
with
\partial_{m}
so that we see the good differential operator
\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}\partial_{m}
, which can only cost
\Lambda_{q}\Gamma_{q}^{13}
from Lemma
3.16
. This is because it can never land on a high-frequency object (any of
\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}
,
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond},\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I},\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}
). In particular, this term can be written in the form appearing in the second equality. We will treat this term similar to the oscillation current error.

We now introduce the following notation:

\displaystyle a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm good}

\displaystyle:=\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}\xi^{n}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)

\displaystyle a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm bad}

\displaystyle:=\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)+\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)\,.

Next, we write the divergence of the second term in (
5.71
) as

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}\left(w_{q+1,\diamond}^{(c)}|w_{q+1,\diamond}^{(p)}|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\epsilon_{mpr}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm bad}+a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm good}\right)\partial_{r}\Phi^{s}(\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{s}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\,a^{2}_{(\xi),\diamond}(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\xi^{n}A_{n}^{j}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=:\mathbf{V}_{3}+\mathbf{V}_{4}\,,

The term inside of the divergence in
\mathbf{V}_{4}
enjoys properties identical to the terms in (
5.70
); indeed, the good differential operator in
a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm good}
only costs
\Lambda_{q}\Gamma_{q}^{13}
,
and so
we absorb these terms into
\phi_{C}^{q+\bar{n},l}
.
On the other hand, we deal with the term
\mathbf{V}_{3}
using (
3.5
) to expand

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{3}

\displaystyle=\partial_{m}\biggl[\epsilon_{mpr}\biggl(\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)\biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{1}{3}\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}\left(-(\xi^{\prime})^{s}\varphi_{\xi}^{{}^{\prime\prime}}+(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\varphi_{\xi}^{\prime}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\,a^{2}_{(\xi),\diamond}(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\xi^{n}A_{n}^{j}\biggr]\,.

Note importantly that this term includes factors of either
\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}
or
\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{n}
from
a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm bad}
and
\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}(\xi^{\prime})^{s}
or
\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}
from
\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I,s}
. We immediately see from the alternating property of the Levi-Civita tensor that the terms including

\epsilon_{mpr}\left(\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}(\xi^{\prime})^{s}+\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{n}\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\right)

vanish. Thus we only have to consider the cross terms, for example the term

\displaystyle\partial_{m}\biggl[\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\frac{1}{3}\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\varphi^{\prime}_{\xi}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\,a^{2}_{(\xi),\diamond}(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\xi^{n}A_{n}^{j}\biggr]\,.

(5.73)

Our first claim is that the vector indexed by
m
inside the parentheses is actually
parallel
to
\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}
, which means that we have the good differential operator! To see this, we write

\displaystyle\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}\partial_{r}\Phi^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\qquad

\displaystyle\parallel\qquad\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}

\displaystyle\iff\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}\partial_{r}\Phi^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\partial_{m}\Phi^{k}\qquad

\displaystyle\parallel\qquad\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}\partial_{m}\Phi^{k}=\xi^{k}

\displaystyle\iff\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}\partial_{r}\Phi^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\partial_{m}\Phi^{k}(\xi^{\prime})^{k}

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}\partial_{r}\Phi^{s}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\partial_{m}\Phi^{k}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{k}=0\,.

But the last two expressions are again equal to zero by the alternating property of the Levi-Civita tensor! Thus we have shown that the
\partial_{m}
on the outside of the expression in (
5.73
) will only cost
\Lambda_{q}\Gamma_{q}^{13}
, and furthermore that it
cannot
land on
\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}
or
\varphi^{\prime}_{\xi}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}
(which is a component of
\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}
). Therefore, we write

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{3}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{C}_{31})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}(\varphi^{\prime}_{\xi}(\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2})\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}+(\mathbf{C}_{32})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}(\varphi^{\prime\prime}_{\xi}(\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2})\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}

(5.74)

where
(\mathbf{C}_{3r})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}
,
r=1,2
, are defined by

\displaystyle(\mathbf{C}_{31})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{3}\xi_{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}\partial_{m}\biggl[\biggl[\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{s}\biggr]_{\parallel\xi}(\xi^{\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}a^{2}_{(\xi),\diamond}(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond})^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\xi^{n}A_{n}^{j}\biggr]

\displaystyle(\mathbf{C}_{32})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{3}\xi_{\ell}A_{\ell}^{m}\partial_{m}\biggl[\biggl[\epsilon_{mpr}\partial_{p}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{n}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{n}(\xi^{\prime})^{s}\biggr]_{\parallel\xi}(\xi^{\prime\prime})^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\partial_{j}\left(a_{(\xi),\diamond}\left(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\partial_{r}\Phi_{(i,k)}^{s}a^{2}_{(\xi),\diamond}(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond})^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\xi^{n}A_{n}^{j}\biggr]\,,

and
[f_{m}]_{\parallel\xi}
denotes the

component of the projection of

onto the vector

. The analysis of

Step 2: Define the current error, pressure increment, and current error, and verify their properties.
Based on the analysis above, we now define the current errors as

\displaystyle\bar{\phi}_{C}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}

\displaystyle:=(\mathcal{H}+\mathcal{R}^{*})\left[(\mathbf{C}_{0})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I,s}\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}_{\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}}\left((\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2}\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I,s}\right)\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathcal{H}+\mathcal{R}^{*})\left[(\mathbf{C}_{31})_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I}\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}_{\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}}(\varphi^{\prime}_{\xi}(\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2})\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\eqref{eq:dc:current:case:1}+\epsilon_{\bullet pr}a_{(\xi),\diamond}^{p,\rm good}\partial_{r}\Phi^{s}(\mathbb{U}_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{s}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\,a^{2}_{(\xi),\diamond}(\boldsymbol{\rho}_{(\xi)}^{\diamond}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\xi}^{I,\diamond}\varrho_{(\xi),\diamond}^{I})^{2}\circ\Phi_{(i,k)}\xi^{\ell}A_{\ell}^{j}\xi^{n}A_{n}^{j}\,.

\mathcal{R}^{*}
or

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i.q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\eqref{eq:dc:current:case:1}\right|\lesssim(\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{{\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+16},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)\,.

and

5.6 Upgrading material derivatives

Definition 5.7

(Definition of
\overline{\phi}_{q+1}
and
\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}
)
.

Recalling Lemmas
5.1
,
3.31
,

,
5.4
,
5.5
, and

, we define
\overline{\phi}_{q+1}=\sum_{m=q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}}\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m}
and
\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m}=\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m,l}+\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m,*}
for
q+1\leq m\leq{q+\bar{n}}
by

\displaystyle\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m,l}

\displaystyle=\overline{\phi}_{O}^{m,l}+\overline{\phi}_{W}^{m,l}+\overline{\phi}^{m,l}_{TNC}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{O}^{m,l}}^{l}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{TN}^{m,l}}^{l}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{C1}^{m,l}}^{l}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{M2}^{m,l}}^{l}+\mathbf{1}_{m={q+\bar{n}}}\overline{\phi}_{L}^{{q+\bar{n}},l}+\mathbf{1}_{m=q+1}\overline{\phi}_{R}^{q+1,l}+\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m,l}

(5.78a)

\displaystyle\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m,*}

\displaystyle=\overline{\phi}_{O}^{m,*}+\overline{\phi}_{W}^{m,*}+\overline{\phi}^{m,*}_{TNC}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{O}^{m,l}}^{*}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{TN}^{m,l}}^{*}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{C1}^{m,l}}^{*}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{M2}^{m,l}}^{*}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{O}^{m,*}}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{TN}^{m,*}}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{C1}^{m,*}}+\overline{\phi}_{S_{M2}^{m,*}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbf{1}_{m={q+\bar{n}}}\overline{\phi}_{L}^{{q+\bar{n}},*}+\overline{\phi}_{R}^{m,*}+\overline{\phi}_{C}^{m,*}+\overline{\phi}_{M}^{m}

(5.78b)

Here, any undefined terms are taken to be
0
. We then define the primitive current error
\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}
by

\displaystyle\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}:=\sum_{m=q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}}\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}^{m}\,,\qquad\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}^{m}=\varphi_{q}^{m}+\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m}\,,

(5.79)

which we note is consistent with (
5.3b
).

Lemma 5.8

(
Upgrading material derivatives).

The new current errors
\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m}=\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m,l}+\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m,*}
satisfy the following. For
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}{{4}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{M}\overline{\phi}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}r_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{i-5},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{11}\right)\,.

(5.80)

For the same range of
N+M
, the current error
\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{q+1}
obeys the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\overline{\phi}^{q+1}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q+1}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{q+1}r_{q+1}^{-1}\Lambda_{m}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+20},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{{10}}\right)\,.

(5.81)

Finally, we have that for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq{q+\bar{n}}
and the same range of
N+M
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\overline{\phi}^{m}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+\mathbf{1}_{m={q+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{{q+\bar{n}}}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{11}\right)\,.

(5.82)

Proof of Lemma
5.8
.

We have that (
5.81
) follows immediately from (
5.12
), (
3.96a
), (
3.100
), (
5.59a
), (
5.59b
), (
3.116a
), (
2.22
), (
2.25
), and (
7.1b
). In order to prove the remaining estimates, we appeal to Lemma
A.3
. The proof is very similar to the proofs of items (
ii
)–(
iv
) of Lemma
3.29
, and so we omit most of the details. The basic idea is however that nonlocal error terms can be upgraded trivially using the minuscule amplitude, and the local error terms can be upgraded using the dodging conclusions that have been included in Lemmas
5.1
,
3.31
,
5.3
,
5.4
,
5.5
, and
3.34
.
∎

6
New intermittent pressure

In this section, we first define a new pressure increment
\sigma_{q+1}
, which leads to a new pressure
p_{q+1}
, and a new intermittent pressure
\pi_{q+1}
. We then replace the Euler-Reynolds system and the relaxed local energy inequality for
(u_{q+1},p_{q},\overline{R}_{q+1},\overline{\varphi}_{q+1},-(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q}))
with the Euler-Reynolds system and relaxed local energy inequality for
(u_{q+1},p_{q+1},R_{q+1},\varphi_{q+1},-\pi_{q+1})
by defining new stress errors
R_{q+1}
and new current errors
\varphi_{q+1}
. In fact adding the pressure increment to the pressure at the
q^{\rm th}
step creates some current errors, called pressure current errors. These will be absorbed into the new current error
\varphi_{q+1}
. This section also verifies all inductive assumptions related to a new intermittent pressure and new stress/current errors, specifically the assumptions in subsection
2.2
,
2.4
, Hypothesis
2.9
, and (
2.44
).

6.1 New pressure increment and new intermittent pressure

In this subsection, we define a new pressure increment
-\sigma_{q+1}=p_{q+1}-p_{q}
and introduce a new intermittent pressure
\pi_{q+1}
.

We collect the pressure increments generated by new errors and new velocity increment potentials. Recall that Lemma
3.19
defined a pressure increment (
\sigma_{\upsilon}
) associated to premollified velocity increment potentials, subsection
3.4
recalled pressure increments (
\sigma_{S^{m}}
) associated to various stress errors, and Lemmas
5.1
,
3.31
, and
5.5
defined pressure increments (
\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{O}^{m}}
,
\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{TN}^{m}}
, and
\sigma_{\overline{\phi}_{C}^{M}}
) associated to various current errors. Then fixing
m
such that
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
, we define

\displaystyle\sigma_{m,q+1}

\displaystyle:=\sigma_{S^{m}}+\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{O}}+\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{TN}}+\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{m={q+\bar{n}}\}}\sigma_{\upsilon}\,.

(6.1)

Recalling that every pressure increment referenced above has a decomposition
\sigma_{\bullet}=\sigma_{\bullet}^{+}-\sigma_{\bullet}^{-}
, we define
\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}
and
\sigma_{m,q+1}^{-}
in the obvious way.

Next, associated to each pressure increment
\sigma_{\bullet}
listed above is a function of time
\mathfrak{m}_{\sigma_{\bullet}}
which satisfies
\mathfrak{m}_{\sigma_{\bullet}}^{\prime}=\langle D_{t,q}\sigma_{\bullet}\rangle
(see Lemma
3.29
and Lemmas
3.19
,
5.2
,
3.32
, and
5.6
), and so we define

\displaystyle\mathfrak{m}_{m,q+1}

\displaystyle:=\mathfrak{m}_{\sigma_{S^{m}}}+\mathfrak{m}_{\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{O}}}+\mathfrak{m}_{\sigma_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{TN}}}+\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{m={q+\bar{n}}\}}\mathfrak{m}_{\sigma_{\upsilon}}\,.

(6.2)

Furthermore, recall that Lemma
3.21
defined a current error associated to velocity pressure increments, subsection
3.4
recalled current errors associated to various stress error pressure increments, and Lemmas
5.2
,
3.32
, and
5.6
defined current errors associated to various current error pressure increments. Then fixing
m,m^{\prime}
such that
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m^{\prime}\leq m\leq{q+\bar{n}}
, we define

\displaystyle\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{m,q+1}

\displaystyle:=\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{{S^{m}}}+\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{O}}}+\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{TN}}}+\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{m={q+\bar{n}}\}}\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{\sigma_{\upsilon}}

(6.3a)

\displaystyle\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{m,q+1}

\displaystyle:=\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{{S^{m}}}+\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{O}}}+\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{TN}}}+\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{m={q+\bar{n}}\}}\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{\upsilon}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbf{1}_{\{m^{\prime}=m\}}\bigg(\phi^{*}_{{S^{m}}}+\phi^{*}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{O}}}+\phi^{*}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{TN}}}+\phi^{*}_{{\overline{\phi}^{m}_{C}}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{m={q+\bar{n}}\}}\phi^{*}_{\upsilon}\bigg)\,.

(6.3b)

Now we set

\phi_{m,q+1}:=\sum_{m^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{m}\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{m,q+1}+\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{m,q+1}\,,

(6.4)

so that the aforementioned lemmas along with (
3.91b
) give the equality

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}\phi_{m,q+1}=D_{t,q}\sigma_{m,q+1}-\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{m,q+1}=D_{t,q}\sigma_{m,q+1}-\langle D_{t,q}\sigma_{q+1,m}\rangle\,.

(6.5)

By appealing to the lemmas mentioned above along with Lemma
3.28
, we have that the
\sigma_{m,q+1}
’s satisfy the properties listed in the following lemma; we refer the reader to Sections
3.4
, and
5
for more details.

Lemma 6.1

(
Collected properties of error terms and pressure increments).

For each
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
,
\sigma_{m,q+1}
satisfies the following properties.

(i)

For any
0\leq k\leq{\mathsf{d}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}S^{m,l}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}}\right)(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(6.6a)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\bar{\phi}^{m,l}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+\mathbf{1}_{m={q+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{{q+\bar{n}}}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(6.6b)

where the first bound holds for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, and the second bound holds for
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}{{4}}
.

(ii)

For
N,M\leq{\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right\|_{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{m}^{-9}\delta_{m+\bar{n}}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(6.7a)

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\Gamma_{m}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-9}}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(6.7b)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}}\right)(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(6.7c)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{-}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim{\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-100}}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)\,.

(6.7d)

(iii)

\sigma_{m,q+1}
and
\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}
have the support properties

\displaystyle B(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}{\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+1}})\cap\sigma_{m,q+1}=\emptyset\qquad\forall q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\,,

(6.8a)

\displaystyle B(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}{\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+1}})\cap\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}=\emptyset\qquad\forall q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq m-1\,.

(6.8b)

Remark 6.2

(
Upgrading material derivatives).

As a consequence of (
6.7a
), (
6.7b
), (
6.7c
), and (
6.8b
),
we may apply Lemma
A.3

to
F=F^{l}=\sigma_{m,q+1}^{\pm}

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right\|_{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\Gamma_{m}^{-9}}\delta_{m+\bar{n}}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{m-1}^{i-5}\tau_{m-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.9a)

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{m}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-9}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{m-1}^{i-5}\tau_{m-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.9b)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}}\right)(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{m-1}^{i-5}\tau_{m-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.9c)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{M}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{-}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim{\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-100}}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}})^{N}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{i-5}\tau_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.9d)

for
{N,M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}}
. Then, applying Lemma
A.1
to
v=\widehat{u}_{m-1}
,
f=\sigma_{m,q+1}^{\pm}
,
p=\infty
, and
\Omega=\mathrm{supp\,}(\psi_{i,m-1})
(or

where
