), we have

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathrm{supp\,}\psi_{i,m-1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{m}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-9}(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N+1}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{m-1}^{i-5}\tau_{m-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.10a)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+{\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}}\right)(\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m})^{N+1}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{m-1}^{i-5}\tau_{m-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.10b)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{M}\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{-}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim{\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-100}}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}})^{N+1}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{i-5}\tau_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\right)\,,

(6.10c)

for
N<\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
and
M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
.

Definition 6.3

(
Pressure increment
\sigma_{q+1}
and decomposition into
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
).

Define constants

\displaystyle a_{m,q,k}:=2^{k-q-1}\left(\frac{\delta_{k+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{m+\bar{n}}}\right){\Gamma_{m}^{9}},\quad A_{m,q}=\sum_{k=m}^{{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}}a_{m,q,k}\,,

(6.11)

where
\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
is chosen in item (
v
) in subsection
7.1
. With these constants in hand, we define

\displaystyle\sigma_{q+1}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\underbrace{A_{m,q}\sigma_{m,q+1}}_{=:\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}},\qquad\sigma_{q+1}^{\pm}:=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\underbrace{A_{m,q}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{\pm}}_{=:\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{\pm}}\,.

(6.12)

Then reorganizing terms in
\sigma_{q+1}
based on amplitude, have that

\displaystyle\sigma_{q+1}=\sum_{k=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\,,

(6.13)

where

\displaystyle\sigma_{q+1}^{q+{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}}=-\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\,,\qquad\sigma_{q+1}^{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{\min(k,q+\bar{n})}a_{m,q,k}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+},\quad\text{for all }q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}\,.

(6.14)

As a direct consequence of Lemma
6.1
and Definition
6.3
, we have that
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
satisfies the following properties.

Lemma 6.4

(
Properties of
\sigma_{q+1}
and
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
).

For all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
, the pressure increment
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
has the following properties.

(i)

\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
has the support property

\displaystyle B(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}{\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+1}})\cap\mathrm{supp\,}(\sigma_{q+1}^{k})=\emptyset\qquad\forall q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\,.

(6.15)

(ii)

For all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}
and
N,M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
, we have that
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\right\|_{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{k+\bar{n}}(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i-3}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma^{-1}_{k-1}\right)\,,

(6.16a)

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{k}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i-3}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma^{-1}_{k-1}\right)\,.

(6.16b)

For all
q+\bar{n}+1\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
and
N,M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
, we have that
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,{q+\bar{n}}}D^{N}D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}}^{M}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\right\|_{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{k+\bar{n}}(\lambda_{{q+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{i-3}\tau_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\Gamma^{-1}_{q+\bar{n}}\right)\,

(6.17a)

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,{q+\bar{n}}}D^{N}D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}}^{M}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}(\lambda_{{q+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{i-3}\tau_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\Gamma^{-1}_{q+\bar{n}}\right)\,.

(6.17b)

(iii)

For
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}
and
0\leq k^{\prime}\leq{\mathsf{d}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}S^{k}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{k}^{-8}\left(\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)\,,

(6.18a)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\bar{\phi}^{k}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{k}^{-13}\left(\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+\mathbf{1}_{m={q+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{{q+\bar{n}}}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q}^{i+18}\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)\,,

(6.18b)

where the first bound holds for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, and the second bound holds for
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}{{4}}
.

(iv)

For
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
and
N,M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
and
q+1\leq k^{\prime}\leq\min(k-1,{q+\bar{n}})
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k^{\prime}}D^{N}D_{t,k^{\prime}}^{M}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+\Gamma_{q}^{-100}\delta_{k+\bar{n}})(\min(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k},\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}))^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k^{\prime}}^{i-3}\tau_{k^{\prime}}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k^{\prime}}^{-1}\Gamma^{-1}_{k^{\prime}}\right)\,.

(6.19)

For the same range of
N
and
M
,
we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{M}\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-25}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}(\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}})^{N}

(6.20)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{i-3}\tau_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.21)

(v)

For all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k\leq k^{\prime}\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{k+\bar{n}}}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\leq 2^{k-k^{\prime}}\sigma_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}\,.

(6.22)

(vi)

For all
q+\bar{n}\leq k^{\prime}\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\leq\sigma_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}.

(6.23)

Proof of Lemma
6.4
.

Proof of item (
i
).
The proof of this item is immediate from Definition
6.3
and item (
iii
) from Lemma
6.1
.

Proof of item (
ii
).
We first consider the estimates for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq k\leq{q+\bar{n}}
. From Remark
6.2
, which ensures that every
\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}
has size
\Gamma_{m}^{-9}\delta_{m+\bar{n}}
in
L^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
, and Definition
6.3
, which ensures that the term in
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
coming from
\sigma_{m,q+1}
has been rescaled by a factor of
\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\delta_{m+\bar{n}}^{-1}\Gamma_{m}^{-9}
, we have that (
6.16a
) holds when
N=M=0
. Similarly, when
N=M=0
, we have that (
6.16b
) holds since
\Gamma_{k}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}
is increasing in
k
. In order to prove the versions of these estimates which involve derivatives, we must use Lemma
A.2
and (
2.19
) (at level
q
since we do not require
D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}-1}
) to upgrade the estimates in Remark
6.2
, since
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
is comprised of rescaled versions of
\sigma_{m,q+1}
for
m\leq k
which came with
D_{t,m-1}
estimates. We omit further details and simply note that the material derivative cost and the assumptions required in (
A.4
) follow from (
2.38
) at level
q
(i.e. we apply (
2.38
) for
q^{\prime}\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
), and that the pointwise bounds follow from the usual trick of choosing
\Omega
to be a neighborhood centered at a point
(x,t)
and then shrinking the diameter of
\Omega
to zero and using continuity. Finally, the proofs of the estimates for
q+\bar{n}+1\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
are quite similar, except that we have to use (
2.19
) and (
2.38
) at level
q+1
(i.e.
q^{\prime}={q+\bar{n}}
), both of which have been already verified in Proposition
3.24
and Proposition
3.25
.

Proof of item (
iii
).

To obtain (
iii
), we use (
6.6a
), (
6.6b
), (
6.11
), and (
6.12
) to give the inequality
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}\geq\sigma_{k,q+1}^{+}2^{k-q-1}\Gamma_{k}^{9}
, nonlocal estimates for
S_{q+1}^{k,*}
from Lemma
3.29
, nonlocal estimates for current errors from Section
5
, and spare factors of
\Gamma_{k}^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}
to absorb implicit constants.

Proof of item (
iv
).
In order to prove (
6.19
), we first prove the estimate when no derivatives have been applied. First note that
\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\delta_{m+\bar{n}}^{-1}\Gamma_{m}^{9}\leq\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q}^{-100}
, since
m\leq{q+\bar{n}}
so that the definition of
\delta_{q^{\prime}}
and (
7.1d
) can absorb
\Gamma_{q}^{-500}
. Then since both sides are linear in
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
, the rescalings involved in the definition of
\sigma_{q+1}^{k}
, (
6.9c
), and the inequality just noted give the proper amplitude bound. At this point we must upgrade material derivative in a manner analogous to that which is required to prove the
L^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
and
L^{\infty}
bounds from item (
ii
) of Lemma
6.4
, and so omit further details. In order to prove (
6.21
), first note that
a_{m,q,k}
is at most
\Gamma_{m}^{10}
if
k=m
and we choose
a_{0}
sufficiently large, and
a_{m,k,q}\ll 1
if
k>m
. Then using (
6.14
), (
6.12
), and the fact that
\Gamma_{q}^{2}>\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}}
since
b^{\bar{n}}<2
from (
2.2b
), the estimate without derivatives follows from (
6.9d
). Upgrading material derivatives then follows in the usual way, and we omit further details.

Proof of item (
v
).
Since
k^{\prime}\geq k
, we have from (
6.14
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{k+\bar{n}}}\sigma_{q+1}^{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{\min(k,q+\bar{n})}\frac{\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{k+\bar{n}}}a_{m,q,k}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{\min(k,q+\bar{n})}2^{k-q-1}\frac{\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{m+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{m}^{9}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}

\displaystyle=2^{k-k^{\prime}}\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{\min(k,q+\bar{n})}a_{m,q,k^{\prime}}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\leq 2^{k-k^{\prime}}\sigma_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}\,.

Proof of item (
vi
).
This estimate follows from the observation in the proof of the previous item that
a_{m,k,q}\ll 1
if
k>m
, (
6.13
), (
6.14
), and a large choice of
a_{0}
which can be used to absorb implicit constants.
∎

Finally, we can define the new intermittent pressure
\pi_{q+1}
.

Definition 6.5

(
New intermittent pressure
\pi_{q+1}
and decomposition into
\pi_{q+1}^{k}
).

We define
\pi_{q+1}^{k}
,
k\geq q+1
, by

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}^{k}

\displaystyle:=\pi_{q}^{k}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\qquad\textnormal{for $q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq k\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}$}\,,\qquad

(6.24)

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}^{k}

\displaystyle:=\pi_{q}^{k}\qquad\textnormal{for $q+1\leq k\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1$, $q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+1\leq k<\infty$}\,.

Then
\pi_{q+1}=\sum_{k=q+1}^{\infty}\pi_{q+1}^{k}
satisfies

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}=\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q}+\sigma_{q+1}+\sum_{k=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\,.

(6.25)

6.2 Inductive assumptions on the new intermittent pressure

In this section, we verify the inductive assumptions on
\pi_{q+1}^{k}
which are required in subsections
2.4
–
2.6
.

Lemma 6.6

(
L^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
,
L^{\infty}
, and pointwise bounds on
\pi_{q+1}^{k}
).

The inductive assumptions (
2.20
) and (
2.21
)
are verified at step
q+1
.

Proof.

We first consider (
2.20a
)–(
2.20c
). In the case that
q+1\leq k\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1
, we have that
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}
from Definition
6.5
, so that the desired estimates follow trivially from inductive assumptions (
2.20a
)–(
2.20c
) at step
q
. In the case that
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}
, we have from Definition
6.5
that
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}
. Therefore, the desired estimates follow from the inductive assumptions and Lemma
6.4
. In order to get (
2.21a
)–(
2.21c
), we have from Definition
6.5
that
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}
. Then the desired estimates follow again from the inductive assumptions and Lemma
6.4
.
∎

Lemma 6.7

(
Lower and upper bounds for
\pi_{q+1}^{k}
).

Inductive assumptions (
2.22
)–(
2.25
) are verified at step
q+1
.

Proof.

In order to prove (
2.22
) at level
q+1
, we first consider the cases when
q+1\leq k\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1
. In these cases the inductive assumption (
2.22
) and Definition
6.5
imply that

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}\geq\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\,.

For the case
k=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, we use (
6.21
) and (
6.24
) to write that

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}=\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+2^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}\geq\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}\,,

concluding the proof of (
2.22
) at level
q+1
. For the remaining cases, we use (
2.25
) at the level of
q+1
, so that we postpone the proof to the end.

Next, from Definition
6.5
, the inductive assumption (
2.23
), and (
6.23
), we have that for
{q+\bar{n}}\leq k^{\prime}<k<q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
,

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}^{k}

\displaystyle=\pi_{q}^{k}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\leq\pi_{q}^{k^{\prime}}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}+\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}\leq\pi_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}\,.

In the endpoint case when
k=q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
, we use that
\pi_{q}^{k+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\equiv\Gamma_{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\delta_{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+\bar{n}}
from (
2.24
), in which case a similar string of inequalities then concludes the proof that (
2.23
) is satisfied at level
q+1
.

From Definition
6.5
and (
2.24
) at level
q
, we have that

\displaystyle\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\Gamma_{k}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}

for
k\geq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+1
, so that the inductive assumption (
2.24
) for
q+1
holds true.

Finally, we must prove (
2.25
) at level
q+1
. We split into cases depending on the value of
q^{\prime\prime}
. If
q^{\prime\prime}\geq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+1
, then we have from Definition
6.5
, the Sobolev inequality applied to (
2.20a
), (
2.21a
), and (
6.16a
), and (
7.6
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\left(\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}\}}\max(0,\sigma_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}})+2^{\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\left(\|\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}+\mathbf{1}_{\{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}\}}\|\sigma_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}\right){+2^{\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}\left(\Gamma_{q}\Gamma_{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{3}+2^{\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\right)\leq\Gamma_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+1}\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q^{\prime}-q^{\prime\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime\prime}}\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}=2^{q^{\prime}-q^{\prime\prime}}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime\prime}}\,.

Note that in the inequalities above, we have assumed a large choice of
a_{0}
to absorb the implicit constant. Next, in the cases when
q+1+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq q^{\prime\prime}\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
, we first note that
\sigma_{q+1}^{q^{\prime\prime}}\geq 0
and
\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\leq 0
since all the minus portions of the pressure have been absorbed into
\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}
in (
6.13
) and (
6.14
). As a consequence of these facts, Definition
6.5
, (
2.25
), and (
6.22
), we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\left(\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}+\mathbf{1}_{\{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}\}}\max\{0,\sigma_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\}{+2^{q^{\prime}-q-1}\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q^{\prime}-q^{\prime\prime}}\left(\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime\prime}}+\max\{0,\sigma_{q+1}^{q^{\prime\prime}}\}{+2^{q^{\prime\prime}-q-1}\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle=2^{q^{\prime}-q^{\prime\prime}}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime\prime}}\,.

Now for the case
q^{\prime\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, we only must consider
q^{\prime}\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1
, and so from Definitions
6.3
and
6.5
and (
6.21
),

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}\leq 2^{q^{\prime}-q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle=\left(\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\right)+\left(\left(2^{q^{\prime}-q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}-1\right)\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}-\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+\left(-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}+\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-25}\right)\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\,.

In the final cases
q^{\prime\prime}<q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, we have from Definition
6.5
and inductive assumption (
2.25
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\delta_{q^{\prime\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}\leq\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime\prime}}=\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime\prime}}\,,

concluding the proof of (
2.25
) at level
q+1
.

Lastly, we consider (
2.22
) for

k>q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
. We first note that from (
2.22
) for
q+1\leq k\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
and (
2.25
) at level
q+1
, we have that for all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k^{\prime}<\infty
,

\pi_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}>2^{k^{\prime}-q-\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\frac{\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}>\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}\,,

and so

\pi_{q+1}^{k}\geq\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\qquad\forall q+1\leq k<\infty\,.

∎

Lemma 6.8

(
Pressure dominates velocity).

The inductive assumptions in (
2.26c
), (
2.29
), and (
2.44
) are verified at level
q+1
.

Proof.

Step 1: Verification of (
2.44
) at level
q+1
.
From (
3.72
) and the definition of
\sigma_{q}^{q+\bar{n}}
in Definition
6.3
, which give an extra prefactor of
\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{9}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q+\bar{n}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q+\bar{n}-1}^{M}\widehat{\upsilon}_{{q+\bar{n}},k^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-4}\left(\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}+\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\times(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{i}\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\right)

for all
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{3\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{2}}
. Then using the definition of
\pi_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}
from Definition
6.5
gives the proof of (
2.44
) for
q^{\prime}={q+\bar{n}}
; in fact we retain the extra smallness prefactor of
\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-4}
, which we shall use in the next step of this proof. In order to verify (
2.44
) for
q\leq q^{\prime}\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
, we appeal to Definition
6.5
for the definition of
\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}
. Noticing that
\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\geq\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}
for all
q^{\prime}\geq q+1
except for
q^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, we have that the verification of (
2.44
) for
q\leq q^{\prime}\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
,
q^{\prime}\neq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
is trivial. In the case
q^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, we use (
6.21
) and (
6.24
) to write that

\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\geq\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\,,

from which (
2.44
) follows using the increase from
\Gamma_{q}
to
\Gamma_{q+1}
in (
2.44
) at level
q
versus level
q+1
, respectively.

Step 2: Verification of (
2.26c
) at level
q+1
.
We first consider the cases
q+1\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}-1
. From the same reasoning as above, which showed that
\pi_{q+1}^{k}\geq\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\pi_{q}^{k}
for
q+1\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}-1
, we have that (
2.26c
) trivially holds. In the case
k={q+\bar{n}}
, we use (
2.42
) at level
q^{\prime}=q+\bar{n}
(verified in Proposition
3.25
) and (
2.44
) for
q^{\prime}=q+\bar{n}
,
k={\mathsf{d}}
(which we just verified with extra factor gain), (
2.45
) at level
q^{\prime}={q+\bar{n}}
(verified in Proposition
3.25
), and (
6.24
) to write that for
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{3\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{2}}

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,{q+\bar{n}}-1}D^{N}D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{M}\widehat{w}_{q+\bar{n}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\psi_{i,{q+\bar{n}}-1}D^{N}D_{t,{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{\upsilon}_{{q+\bar{n}},{\mathsf{d}}}+\widehat{e}_{{q+\bar{n}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-3}\left((\pi_{q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}\right)(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{i}\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(\pi_{q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{i}\tau_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}-1}^{-1}\right)\,,

which verifies (
2.26c
) at level
q+1
with
q^{\prime}={q+\bar{n}}
.

Step 3: Verification of (
2.29
) for
q^{\prime}=q+\bar{n}
.
We will prove that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{{i_{\rm max}}}\psi_{i,q+\bar{n}}^{2}\delta_{q+\bar{n}}r_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{2i}

\displaystyle\lesssim{r_{q}^{-2}}\pi_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\,

(6.26)

for a
q
-independent implicit constant, from which (
2.29
) for
q^{\prime}=q+\bar{n}
follows by using the extra factor of
\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}
to absorb the implicit constant and the powers of
2
.

Note that from (
6.1
) and the subsequent sentence, which shows that
\sigma_{{q+\bar{n}},q+1}^{+}\geq\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}
, Definition
6.3
, which shows that
\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\geq\sigma_{{q+\bar{n}},q+1}^{+}
, and Definition
6.5
, which shows that
\pi_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\geq\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}
, we have that
\sigma_{\upsilon}^{+}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}\leq\pi_{q+1}^{q+\bar{n}}
. Thus, using (
6.24
), we see that (
6.26
) follows from (
3.73
) of Lemma
3.26
and, consequently, we have (
2.29
) at level
q+1
with
q^{\prime}={q+\bar{n}}
.

Step 4: Verification of (
2.29
) for
q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
.
Recall that in
Step 1
, we showed that

\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\geq\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}\qquad q^{\prime}\geq q+1\,.

(6.27)

Therefore we may use (
2.29
) at level
q
to write that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{{i_{\rm max}}}\psi_{i,q^{\prime}}^{2}\delta_{q^{\prime}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}^{2i}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q-q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-2}\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=2^{q+1-q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-2}\left(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\pi_{q}^{q^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{q+1-q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-2}\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\,,

concluding the proof of (
2.29
) at level
q+1
.
∎

Lemma 6.9

(
Pressure dodging at level
q+1
).

Hypothesis
2.9
is verified at step
q+1
.

Proof.

We must show that for all
q+1<k\leq q+\bar{n}
,
k\leq k^{\prime}
, and
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

We divide up the proof into cases based on the value of
k^{\prime}
.

Case 1:
q+1<k\leq k^{\prime}<q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
.
From (
6.24
), we have that
\pi_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}=\pi_{q}^{k^{\prime}}
, and so using Hypothesis
2.9
at level
q
and (
6.27
), we have that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q+1}^{k^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q}^{k^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{q}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

Case 2:
q+1<k\leq k^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
.
In this case, we have from (
6.24
) that
\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}=\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+2^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}
.
Then considering just the contribution
\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}
to
\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}
from the first term, we have from Hypothesis
2.9
at level
q
and (
6.27
) that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{q}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

Next, we have from (
6.15
) that
\widehat{w}_{k}\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\equiv 0
for
q+1<k\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
, and so we may ignore the contribution from
\sigma_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}
. Finally, in order to bound the contribution coming from the constant term
\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}
, we use (
2.22
) and (
2.26c
) at level
q+1
and (
7.1c
) to write that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}2^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+\bar{n}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}\Gamma_{q+1}r_{k-\bar{n}}^{-1}(\pi_{q+1}^{k})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

Case 3:
q+1<k\leq k^{\prime}
,
q+1<k\leq{q+\bar{n}}
, and
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k^{\prime}<\infty
.
From Definition
6.5
, we have that in these cases, either
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}
or
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}
. We therefore first make a few preliminary calculations to help bound the contributions from
\pi_{q}^{k}
and
2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}
before dividing up further into subcases. We first recall (
2.22
) at level
q+1
,

\pi_{q+1}^{k}\geq\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\qquad\forall q+1\leq k<\infty\,.

(6.29)

Then, we have from (
2.26c
) at level
q+1
that for all
q+1<k\leq k^{\prime}\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
,
k\leq k^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\delta_{k^{\prime}+\bar{n}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+1}r_{k-\bar{n}}^{-1}(\pi_{q+1}^{k})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.30)

Next, we have from Hypothesis
2.9
at level
q
and (
6.27
) that for
q+1<k\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
and
k\leq k^{\prime}<\infty
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\pi_{q}^{k^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{q}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-100}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.31)

We claim that the above estimate holds in addition for
k={q+\bar{n}}
and
k\leq k^{\prime}<\infty
. Indeed from (
2.21c
) and (
2.23
) at level
q
, (
2.26c
) at level
q+1
, (
6.27
), and (
7.1c
), we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q+\bar{n}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q+\bar{n}-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{{q+\bar{n}}}\pi_{q}^{k^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q+1}r_{q}^{-1}(\pi_{q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q}\pi_{q}^{k^{\prime}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{q+1}{\Gamma_{k}^{-101}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{i+{1}}\tau_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.32)

Finally, we use Definition
6.3
, equations (
6.11
)–(
6.14
) and the dodging ensured by (
6.8b
) to write that for
q+1<k\leq{q+\bar{n}}
,
k\leq k^{\prime}
, and
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k^{\prime}<\infty
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(\widehat{w}_{k}\sigma_{q+1}^{\min(k^{\prime},q+\bar{n})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{k^{\prime}}\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(a_{m,q,k^{\prime}}\widehat{w}_{k}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{k}\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\left(a_{m,q,k^{\prime}}\widehat{w}_{k}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right)\right|\,.

Then using (
2.26c
) and (
2.19
) at level
q+1
, (
6.7c
), and (
6.8b
), we have that the quantity above is controlled by

\displaystyle\sum_{N_{1}=0}^{N}\sum_{M_{1}=0}^{M}\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N_{1}}D_{t,k-1}^{M_{1}}\widehat{w}_{k}\right|\left|\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{k}a_{m,q,k^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{supp\,}\psi_{i,k-1}}D^{N-N_{1}}D_{t,k-1}^{M-M_{1}}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{N_{1},M_{1}}\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N_{1}}D_{t,k-1}^{M_{1}}\widehat{w}_{k}\right|\sum_{i^{\prime}:\psi_{i^{\prime},q}\psi_{i,k-1}\not\equiv 0}\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{k}a_{m,q,k}\mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{supp\,}\psi_{i^{\prime},q}}\left|D^{N-N_{1}}D_{t,q}^{M-M_{1}}\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q+1}r_{k-\bar{n}}^{-1}(\pi_{q+1}^{k})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}(\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+\Gamma_{q}^{-100}\delta_{k+\bar{n}})\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{k}^{-101}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{k}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{k}^{-1}\mathrm{T}_{k}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.33)

Here, the second inequality follows from
0\leq a_{m,q,k^{\prime}}\leq a_{m,q,k}
because of
k\leq k^{\prime}
, and the third inequality follows from the proof of (
6.19
).

Case 3a:
q+1<k\leq{q+\bar{n}}
,
k\leq k^{\prime}
, and
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k^{\prime}\leq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
.
In these cases, we have from Definition
6.5
that
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}+\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}
. Then combining (
6.30
), (
6.31
), (
6.32
), and (
6.33
) concludes the proof.

Case 3b:
q+1<k\leq{q+\bar{n}}
,
k\leq k^{\prime}
, and
q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+1\leq k^{\prime}<\infty
.
In these cases, we have from Definition
6.5
that
\pi_{q+1}^{k}=\pi_{q}^{k}
. Then (
6.31
) gives the desired estimate.
∎

6.3 The Euler-Reynolds system and the relaxed LEI adapted to new pressure

In this section, we upgrade the Euler-Reynold system (
3.75
) and the relaxed local energy inequality (
5.8
) adapted to the new pressure
p_{q+1}=p_{q}-\sigma_{q+1}
and the new intermittent pressure
\pi_{q+1}
defined in subsections
6.1
.

Lemma 6.10

(Relaxed equations at level
q+1
)
.

The inductive assumptions (
2.7
)–(
2.12
) are satisfied at level
q+1
.

Proof.

We first set a few notations and definitions. Referring to (
6.2
), (
6.3
), and Definition (
6.5
), we first define
\phi_{P1}
and
\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}
by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}:=\frac{3}{2}\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{q+\bar{n}}A_{m,q}\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{m,q+1}\,,\qquad\phi_{P1}:=\frac{3}{2}\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{q+\bar{n}}A_{m,q}\phi_{m,q+1}\,.

(6.34)

From the definition (
6.12
) of
\sigma_{q+1}
and (
6.5
), we therefore have that

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}D_{t,q}\sigma_{q+1}=\mathrm{div}\phi_{P1}+\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}\,.

(6.35)

In particular,
\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}=\frac{3}{2}\langle D_{t,q}\sigma_{q+1}\rangle
. Recalling (
3.79
) and (
5.7
), we now define

R^{q+\bar{n},*}_{q+1}=\overline{R}^{q+\bar{n},*}_{q+1}+\frac{2}{3}(\mathfrak{m}_{T}+\mathfrak{m}_{N}+\mathfrak{m}_{L}+\mathfrak{m}_{M1}+\mathfrak{m}_{M2}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1})\mathrm{Id}\,,

(6.36)

and we do not modify the local part of the Reynolds stress that was defined in (
3.79
), nor do we modify any of the nonlocal portions for
q+1\leq m\leq{q+\bar{n}}-1
. We then define a new stress error at step
q+1
by

\displaystyle R_{q+1}

\displaystyle=\overline{R}_{q+1}+\frac{2}{3}(\mathfrak{m}_{T}+\mathfrak{m}_{N}+\mathfrak{m}_{L}+\mathfrak{m}_{M1}+\mathfrak{m}_{M2}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1})\mathrm{Id}\underset{\eqref{eq:meanz:def}}{=}\overline{R}_{q+1}+\frac{2}{3}\left(\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1}\right)\mathrm{Id}\,,

(6.37)

which verifies (
2.10
).

Recalling (
6.25
), (
3.75
) and the new pressure

\displaystyle p_{q+1}

\displaystyle=-\sigma_{q+1}+{p_{q}}\,,

(6.38)

we now have that

(u_{q+1},p_{q+1},R_{q+1},-\pi_{q+1})
solves the Euler-Reynolds system

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u_{q+1}+\mathrm{div}\left(u_{q+1}\otimes u_{q+1}\right)+\nabla{p_{q+1}}=\mathrm{div}(-\pi_{q+1}\mathrm{Id}+R_{q+1})\,,

(6.39)

where we have used that the constant term in (
6.25
) and the terms with functions of time
\mathfrak{m}_{\bullet}
in
R_{q+1}
vanish inside of the divergence. Thus we have verified (
2.7
). Note that we have also verified (
2.11
) as well. Recalling (
3.71
), we have in addition that (
2.9
) is verified, and so it only remains to check (
2.12
) and (
2.8
) at level
q+1
.

Let us set

\displaystyle\kappa_{q+1}=\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}(R_{q+1}-\pi_{q+1}\mathrm{Id}))\,.

(6.40)

Then we can now rewrite (
5.8
) as the relaxed local energy inequality for
(u_{q+1},p_{q+1},R_{q+1},-\pi_{q+1},\varphi_{q+1})
adapted to the upgraded stress error
R_{q+1}
and the new pressure
\pi_{q+1}
. Specifically, we have that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\frac{1}{2}|u_{q+1}|^{2}\right)+\mathrm{div}\left(\left(\frac{1}{2}|u_{q+1}|^{2}+p_{q+1}\right)u_{q+1}\right)

\displaystyle\underset{\eqref{eq:LEI:new},\eqref{new:pressure}}{=}(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)(\overline{\kappa}_{q+1}+\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}})+\mathrm{div}\left({(\overline{R}_{q+1}-(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q})\mathrm{Id})}\widehat{u}_{q+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathrm{div}\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}-E-\mathrm{div}\left(\sigma_{q+1}u_{q+1}\right)

\displaystyle\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\eqref{eq:def:ov:kappa},\\
\mathrm{div}\widehat{u}_{q+1}\equiv 0\\
\nabla\mathfrak{m}_{\bullet}\equiv 0\end{subarray}}{=}(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)\left(\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{tr\,}\left(\overline{R}_{q+1}-\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q}+\sigma_{q+1}+\sum_{k=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}-\frac{2}{3}\left(\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1}\right)\right)\mathrm{Id}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathrm{div}\left(\left((\overline{R}_{q+1}-\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q}^{q}+\sigma_{q+1}+\sum_{k=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}2^{k-q-1}\delta_{k+\bar{n}}-\frac{2}{3}\left(\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1}\right)\right)\mathrm{Id}\right)\widehat{u}_{q+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathrm{div}\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}-E-\mathrm{div}\left(\sigma_{q+1}(u_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q+1})\right)+\frac{3}{2}(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)\sigma_{q+1}-\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}

\displaystyle\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}\eqref{defn:new.pr},\eqref{billystrings:eq:1},\\
\eqref{billys:1}\end{subarray}}{=}(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)\kappa_{q+1}+\mathrm{div}\left({(R_{q+1}-\pi_{q+1}\mathrm{Id})}\widehat{u}_{q+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathrm{div}\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}-E-\mathrm{div}\left(\sigma_{q+1}(u_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q+1})\right)+\frac{3}{2}\underbrace{(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)}_{=\partial_{t}+(\widehat{u}_{q}+\widehat{w}_{q+1})\cdot\nabla}\sigma_{q+1}-\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}

\displaystyle\underset{\eqref{supp.sik},\eqref{eq:rewriting:new:si}}{=}(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)\kappa_{q+1}+\mathrm{div}\left({(R_{q+1}-\pi_{q+1}\mathrm{Id})}\widehat{u}_{q+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathrm{div}\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}-E-\underbrace{\mathrm{div}(\sigma_{q+1}(u_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q+1}))}_{=:\mathrm{div}\phi_{P2}}+\underbrace{\frac{3}{2}(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q}\cdot\nabla)\sigma_{q+1}-\mathfrak{m}^{\prime}_{P1}}_{=\mathrm{div}\phi_{P1}}

\displaystyle\qquad=(\partial_{t}+\widehat{u}_{q+1}\cdot\nabla)\kappa_{q+1}+\mathrm{div}\left({(R_{q+1}-\pi_{q+1}\mathrm{Id})}\widehat{u}_{q+1}\right)+\underbrace{\mathrm{div}\varphi_{q+1}}_{=\mathrm{div}(\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}+\phi_{P1}+\phi_{P2})}-E\,.

(6.41)

Thus we have verified (
2.8
), and recalling (
5.79
), we have that (
2.12
) is verified as well.
∎

6.4 Pressure current error

In this subsection, we analyze the new pressure current errors
\phi_{P1}
and
\phi_{P2}
defined in (
6.41
).

6.4.1 Pressure current error I

Recalling the definition of
\phi_{m,q+1}
from (
6.3
)–(
6.5
) and the definition of
\phi_{P1}
from (
6.34
), we decompose
\phi_{P1}
into

\displaystyle\phi_{P1}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2}\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{q+\bar{n}}\sum_{m^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{m}\left(A_{m,q}{\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{m,q+1}}+A_{m,q}{\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{m,q+1}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{q+\bar{n}}\left(\sum_{m=m^{\prime}}^{q+\bar{n}}A_{m,q}\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{m,q+1}+\sum_{m=m^{\prime}}^{q+\bar{n}}A_{m,q}\phi^{m^{\prime},*}_{m,q+1}\right)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{m^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}}^{q+\bar{n}}\phi^{m^{\prime},l}_{P1}+\phi_{P1}^{m^{\prime},*}\,.

(6.42)

Lemma 6.11

(
Properties of
\phi_{P1}
).

For all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
, the terms
\phi_{P1}^{m,l}
and
\phi_{P1}^{m,*}
satisfy the following properties.

(i)

The local part
\phi_{P1}^{m,l}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\phi_{P1}^{m,l}\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{m}^{-80}(\pi_{q}^{m})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}{r_{m}^{-1}}\Lambda_{m}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+18},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{11}\right)

(6.43a)

\displaystyle\mathrm{supp\,}\phi_{P1}^{m,l}

\displaystyle\cap B(\mathrm{supp\,}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}},\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}{\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}})=\emptyset,\qquad q+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq m-1

(6.43b)

for all
N,M<\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
.

(ii)

For all
N,M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, the non-local part
\phi_{P1}^{m,*}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{i,q}D^{N}D_{t,q}^{M}\phi_{P1}^{m,*}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{2\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\Lambda_{m}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{9}\right)

(6.44)

Remark 6.12
.

Applying Lemma
A.3
to
F^{\ell}=\phi_{P1}^{k,l}
and
F^{*}=\phi_{P1}^{k,*}
, for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}{+1}\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}
we can upgrade the material derivatives in the estimates (
6.43a
) to obtain that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\phi_{P1}^{k}\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{k}^{-60}(\pi_{q}^{k})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}{r_{k}^{-1}}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{k-1}^{i},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\right)\,.

(6.45)

Proof of Lemma
6.11
.

From the definition (
6.11
) of
A_{m,q}
, we have that
A_{m,q}\leq\Gamma_{m}^{10}
. Recalling the definition (
6.3
) of
\phi^{m^{\prime}}_{m,q+1}
, it is immediate that
\phi^{m^{\prime}}_{m,q+1}
satisfy the same properties delineated in Lemma
3.21
(for the current errors associated to velocity pressure increments), Lemma
3.30
(for the current errors associated to stress error pressure increments), and Lemmas
5.2
,
3.32
, and
5.6
(for the current errors associated to current error pressure increments). Therefore, the lemma follows from the definition (
6.42
).
∎

6.4.2 Pressure current error II

Here we deal with the error

\displaystyle\mathrm{div}\phi_{P2}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{div}(\sigma_{q+1}(u_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q+1}))\,.

Recall from (
2.9
) at level
q+1
that

u_{q+1}-\widehat{u}_{q+1}=\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+2}^{q+\bar{n}}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}\,.

By the definition of
\sigma_{q+1}
in (
6.12
) and the dodging properties (
6.8a
), (
6.8b
), and (
6.15
), we first write

\displaystyle\sigma_{q+1}\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+2}^{q+\bar{n}}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\sigma_{q+1}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\sum_{q^{\prime}=m+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}+\sum_{m=q+{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}\widehat{w}_{m}-\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}\,.

Since
\mathrm{div}\left(\sigma_{q+1}\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+2}^{q+\bar{n}}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}\right)
has zero mean, we recall the identity (
2.42
) at level
q+1
and set

\displaystyle\phi^{q^{\prime}}_{P2}

\displaystyle:=\underbrace{\left[\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q^{\prime}-1}(\mathcal{H}+\mathcal{R}^{*})(\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}\cdot(\mathrm{div}^{\mathsf{d}}\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}}))\right]}_{=:\phi_{P21}^{q^{\prime}}}+\underbrace{\widetilde{\sigma}_{q^{\prime},q+1}^{+}\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}}}_{=:\phi_{P22}^{q^{\prime}}}-\underbrace{(\mathcal{H}+\mathcal{R}^{*})(\nabla\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\cdot(\mathrm{div}^{\mathsf{d}}\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}}))}_{=:\phi_{P23}^{q^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\left[\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q^{\prime}-1}\mathcal{R}^{*}(\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}\cdot\widehat{e}_{q^{\prime}})\right]-\mathcal{R}^{*}(\nabla\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\cdot\widehat{e}_{q^{\prime}})}_{=:\phi_{P*}^{q^{\prime}}}

(6.46)

\displaystyle\phi_{P2}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\phi_{P2}^{q^{\prime}}\,.

As before, the terms including
\mathcal{R}^{*}
are non-local and the rest are local.

Lemma 6.13

(
Properties of
\sigma_{P2}^{m}
).

For
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
, the error
\phi^{m}_{P2}
has no pressure increment; for
N+M\leq{2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}
, it satisfies

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}\phi^{m}_{P2}\right|

\displaystyle<\Gamma_{m}^{-80}\left(\pi^{m}_{q+1}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}\Lambda_{m}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{i+3},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{2}\right)\,.

(6.47)

Proof.

We divide the proof up into cases based on the decomposition in (
6.46
).

Step 1: Estimate of
\phi_{P22}^{m}
.
By (
6.9c
), the definition of
A_{m,q}
and
\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}
in Definition
6.3
, Definition
6.5
, which shows that
\pi_{q+1}^{m}\geq\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\geq\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}\Gamma_{m}^{-25}
, (
2.26c
) at level
q+1
to bound
\widehat{w}_{m}
, and (
7.1c
) to absorb errant factors of
\Gamma_{m}
, we have that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
and
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N}D_{t,m-1}^{M}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}\widehat{w}_{m})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}N_{1}+N_{2}=N\\
M_{1}+M_{2}=M\end{subarray}}\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N_{1}}D_{t,m-1}^{M_{1}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|\left|\psi_{i,m-1}D^{N_{2}}D_{t,m-1}^{M_{2}}\widehat{w}_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}})\left(\pi_{q+1}^{m}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+1}r_{m-\bar{n}}^{-1}\Lambda_{m}^{N}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{i},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{m}^{-101}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{m}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{m}^{-1}\Lambda_{m}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{i},\mathrm{T}_{m-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{m-1}^{2}\right)\,.

Therefore,
\phi_{P22}^{m}
satisfies the desired pointwise estimate.

Step 2: Estimates of
\phi_{P21}^{q^{\prime}}
,
\phi_{P23}^{q^{\prime}}
, and
\phi_{P*}^{q^{\prime}}
.
We first carry out the preliminary step of upgrading material derivatives on
\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{\pm}
, which will be required for all three terms. We apply Lemma
A.2
inductively to
v=\widehat{u}_{m-1}
,
f=\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{\pm}
, and
w=\widehat{w}_{m},\dots,\widehat{w}_{q^{\prime}-1}
, and
\Omega=\mathrm{supp\,}(\psi_{i,q^{\prime}-1})
. The assumptions in the remark are satisfied due to
(
2.38
), Remark
6.2
, and (
2.36
). As a result, we have that for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq q^{\prime}-1
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q^{\prime}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}+\delta_{q+3\bar{n}})\Lambda_{q^{\prime}-1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.48a)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q^{\prime}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\nabla\sigma_{m,q+1}^{+}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-100}\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Lambda_{q^{\prime}-1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.48b)

for
N,M<\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
. In a similar way, we have

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{q+1}^{m,+}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+2}\Lambda_{q^{\prime}-1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{{i_{\rm max}}+3},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.49a)

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\nabla\sigma^{-}_{q+1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-50}\Lambda_{q^{\prime}-1}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{{i_{\rm max}}+3},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.49b)

for
N,M<\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{200}}
and
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
. The first inequality holds for
m+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\bar{n}
, while the second one holds for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\bar{n}
.

Step 2a:
We now estimate
\phi_{P21}^{m^{\prime}}
by applying Lemma
A.12
with

\displaystyle G=\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{m,q+1}^{+},\quad\vartheta=\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}},\quad\pi=\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}^{25}\pi_{q+1}^{m},\quad\pi^{\prime}=\Gamma_{q+1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}(\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-1}\,,\quad M_{t}=\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\quad v=\widehat{u}_{q^{\prime}-1},

\displaystyle\Omega=\mathrm{supp\,}(\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}}\psi_{i,q^{\prime}-1})\,,\quad\lambda=\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m},\quad\Upsilon=\lambda_{q^{\prime}},\quad\Lambda=\lambda_{q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}},\quad\nu=\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\quad\nu^{\prime}=\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{G,\infty}=\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+1},\quad\quad\mathcal{C}_{*,\infty}=\Gamma_{q+1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}(\Gamma_{q+1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+1})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-1}\,,\quad{\mathsf{d}}\textnormal{ as in \eqref{i:par:10}/\eqref{exp.w.q+1}}\,,

\displaystyle M_{\circ}=N_{\circ}=2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}\,,\quad K_{\circ}\textnormal{ as in \eqref{ineq:K_0}}\,,\quad N_{*}=M_{*}=\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{300}}\,.

Then we have that (
A.23b
) is satisfied due to (
2.38
) at level
q+1
, (
A.44a
) is satisfied due to and Definitions
6.5
and
6.3
and (
6.10b
), (
A.44b
) is satisfied due to (
2.44
) at level
q+1
, all assumptions from item (
i
) in Part 4 of Proposition
A.7
are satisfied due to Remark
A.8
, (
A.48a
) and (
A.48b
) are satisfied due to (
2.20
) at level
q+1
, and (
A.49
) is satisfied due to (
7.12b
). Then from (
A.47
), (
7.1c
), (
xii
), and (
2.25
) at level
q+1
, we have that for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq m\leq q+\bar{n}
and
m+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\bar{n}
and
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q^{\prime}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\mathcal{H}(\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{q+1}^{m,+}\cdot\mathrm{div}^{{\mathsf{d}}}\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}})\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\lambda_{m}\Gamma_{m}^{25}\pi_{q+1}^{m}\cdot\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-1}(\lambda_{q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}^{-100}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\lambda_{q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{2}\right)\,.

From (
A.51
) and for the same range of
N
and
M
, we also have that

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}(\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{q+1}^{m,+}\cdot\mathrm{div}^{{\mathsf{d}}}\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}}^{-100}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{\frac{3}{2}}(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{{q+\bar{n}-1}}^{-1}\Gamma_{{q+\bar{n}}-1}^{2}\right)\,,

concluding the proof of the desired estimates for
\phi_{P21}^{q^{\prime}}
.

Step 2b:
In the case of
\phi_{P23}^{q^{\prime}}
, we instead set

\displaystyle G=\nabla\sigma_{q+1}^{-},\quad\mathcal{C}_{G,\infty}=\Gamma_{q+1}\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+2},\quad\lambda=\lambda^{\prime}=\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}},\quad\pi=\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{25}\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\,,

while the remaining parameters stay the same. Concluding again as before, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,q^{\prime}-1}D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\mathcal{H}(\nabla\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\cdot\mathrm{div}^{\mathsf{d}}\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}})\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\pi_{q+1}^{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\cdot\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q^{\prime}-\bar{n}}^{-1}\lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}^{-100}\left(\pi_{q+1}^{q^{\prime}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\left(\lambda_{q^{\prime}}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}\right)^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{2}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}(\nabla\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\cdot\mathrm{div}^{\mathsf{d}}\widehat{\upsilon}_{q^{\prime}})\right\|_{\infty}\leq\Gamma_{q^{\prime}}^{-100}\delta_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}^{\frac{3}{2}}r_{q^{\prime}}^{-1}\lambda_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}-1}^{i+3}\tau_{{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}-1}}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}-1}^{2}\right)\,,

for
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq q^{\prime}\leq q+\bar{n}
, where the range of
N
and
M
are the same as before.

Step 2c:
Finally, we must estimate
\phi_{P*}^{q^{\prime}}
. By Remark
A.11
and using (
6.49
), (
2.45
), and (
2.45
) for
q\mapsto q+1
(verified in Proposition

), we have that for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{m=q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{q+\bar{n}}\sum_{q^{\prime}=m+1}^{q+\bar{n}}

\displaystyle\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}(\nabla\widetilde{\sigma}_{q+1}^{m,+}\cdot\widehat{e}_{q^{\prime}})\right\|_{\infty}+\sum_{q^{\prime}=q+\lfloor\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\rfloor+1}^{q+\bar{n}}\left\|D^{N}D_{t,q^{\prime}-1}^{M}\mathcal{R}^{*}(\nabla\sigma_{q+1}^{-}\cdot\widehat{e}_{q^{\prime}})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-100}\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}^{\frac{3}{2}}r_{q}^{-1}\Lambda_{q^{\prime}}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q^{\prime}-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{q^{\prime}-1}^{2}\right)\,.

∎

6.5 Inductive estimates on the new errors

Lemma 6.14

(
Inductive pointwise error estimates).

The inductive assumptions (
2.26a
), (
2.26b
), and (
2.28
)
are satisfied at level
q+1
.

Proof.

We recall from (
3.78
) and (
6.37
) the definition of the stress error

R_{q+1}=\overline{R}_{q+1}+\frac{2}{3}\left(\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1}\right)\mathrm{Id}=\sum_{m=q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}}\left(R_{q}^{m}+S_{q+1}^{m}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\left(\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1}\right)\mathrm{Id}\,.

Recall also the definition of

\varphi_{q+1}=\overline{\varphi}_{q+1}+\phi_{P1}+\phi_{P2}=\sum_{m=q+1}^{{q+\bar{n}}}\left(\varphi_{q}^{m}+\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{m}\right)+\phi_{P1}+\phi_{P2}

from (
5.79
), (
6.41
), (
6.42
), and (
6.46
). We therefore define the new current errors
\phi_{q+1}^{k}
by

\displaystyle\varphi_{q+1}^{k}=\bar{\varphi}_{q+1}^{k}+\phi_{P1}^{k}+\phi_{P2}^{k}\,.

In order to prove (
2.26a
) and (
2.26b
) at level
q+1
, we first consider the cases
q+1\leq k\leq q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}
. Recall from Lemma
3.29
and Lemma
5.8
that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}S_{q+1}^{k}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma^{-10}_{k}\pi_{q}^{k}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+19}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{k-1}^{9}\right)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}\overline{\phi}_{q+1}^{k}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Gamma^{-10}_{k}(\pi_{q}^{k})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}\Lambda_{k}^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+19}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{k-1}^{9}\right)\,,

where the first bound holds for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, the second holds for
N+M\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}{{4}}
, and we have used the lower bound on
\pi_{q}^{k}
given in (
2.22
). Then (
2.26a
) and (
2.26b
) at level
q+1
follow from the definitions recalled at the beginning of the proof, (
2.26a
) and (
2.26b
) at level
q
, the estimates just recorded, and (
6.27
).

In the cases when
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}
, we upgrade the material derivatives in (
6.18a
) and (
6.19
) applying Lemma
A.3
to
F:=S_{q+1}^{k}=S_{q+1}^{k,l}+S_{q+1}^{k,*}=:F^{l}+F^{*}
and
F:=\bar{\phi}_{q+1}^{k}=\bar{\phi}_{q+1}^{k,l}+\bar{\phi}_{q+1}^{k,*}=:F^{l}+F^{*}
, obtaining that

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,k-1}D^{N}D_{t,k-1}^{M}S^{k}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{k}^{-8}\left(\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+\delta_{k+\bar{n}}\right)(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i+18}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{k-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.50a)

\displaystyle\left|\psi_{i,{k-1}}D^{N}D_{t,{k-1}}^{M}\overline{\phi}^{k}_{q+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{k}^{-13}\left(\sigma_{q+1}^{k}+\mathbf{1}_{m={q+\bar{n}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{{q+\bar{n}}}+\mathbf{1}_{m=q+1}\Gamma_{q+1}^{-50}\pi_{q}^{q+1}\delta_{q+2\bar{n}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}r_{k}^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times(\lambda_{k}\Gamma_{k})^{N}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\Gamma_{k-1}^{i}\tau_{k-1}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{k-1}^{-1}\Gamma_{k-1}^{-1}\right)

(6.50b)

for
N+M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
. In addition, recalling the definitions in (
6.2
), (
6.34
), and (
5.7
), and the estimates given in Lemmas
3.19
,
3.28
,
5.2
,
3.31
,
3.32
,
5.3
, and
5.6
, we have that for
M\leq 2\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
,

\left|\frac{d^{M}}{dt^{M}}\left(\mathfrak{m}_{\overline{\phi}_{q+1}}+\mathfrak{m}_{P1}\right)\right|\leq\delta_{q+\nicefrac{{5\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\mathcal{M}\left(M,\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}},\tau_{q}^{-1},\mathrm{T}_{q+1}^{-1}\right)\,.

Then, using (
6.45
), (
6.47
), Definitions
6.3
and
6.5
, and (
6.27
), we conclude the proofs of (
2.26a
) and (
2.26b
).

Finally, we note that the nonlocal bounds in (
2.28
) follow from (
6.36
), (
3.88
) from Lemma
3.29
, and the estimate just above.
∎

7
Parameters

In this section, we treat various parameters appearing in the proof that were not previously specified in subsection
2.1
. We also record several associated consequences of parameter choices. For further details including the arithmetic which justifies the validity of various choices, we refer the reader to
[
25
, Section 11]
.

7.1 Definitions and inequalities

(i)

Recalling the definition of
\Gamma_{q}
in (
2.4
) and using the definitions in section
2.1
, we may now choose
0<\varepsilon_{\Gamma}\ll(b-1)^{2}<1
sufficiently small so that a number of conditions hold. For the full list of these conditions, we refer to
[
25
, equation 11.10]
. The ones which will be relevant for this paper are

\displaystyle\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{3}\Gamma_{q}^{-2}\frac{\lambda_{q^{\prime}+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}}{\lambda_{q}\lambda_{q^{\prime}+\bar{n}}}

\displaystyle\leq 1\qquad\textnormal{for all $q^{\prime}$ such that $q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1-\bar{n}\leq q^{\prime}\leq q$}\,,

(7.1a)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\lambda_{q}}{\lambda_{q^{\prime}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{2000+10{\mathsf{C}_{b}}}

\displaystyle<\left(\frac{\delta_{q}}{\delta_{q^{\prime}}}\right)^{-1}

(7.1b)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{r_{q+1}}{r_{q}}\right)\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{1000+10{\mathsf{C}_{b}}}

\displaystyle\leq 1

(7.1c)

\displaystyle\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{1000}

\displaystyle<\min\left(\lambda_{q}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}r_{q}^{-2},\lambda_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}\lambda_{q+1}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}},\delta_{q}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}\delta_{q+1}^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}\right)

(7.1d)

\displaystyle\left\lceil\frac{(b^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}+\dots+b+1)^{2}}{\varepsilon_{\Gamma}(b^{\bar{n}-1}+\dots+b+1)}\right\rceil

\displaystyle\geq 20\,,\qquad 2000\varepsilon_{\Gamma}b^{\bar{n}}<1\,.

(7.1e)

(ii)

Choose
\mathsf{C_{\infty}}
as

\mathsf{C_{\infty}}=3\left\lceil\frac{(b^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}-1)^{2}}{(b-1)^{2}\varepsilon_{\Gamma}(b^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1}+\dots+b+1)}+\frac{2000b^{\bar{n}}}{b^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}-1}+\frac{4b^{\bar{n}-1}}{(b-1)\varepsilon_{\Gamma}(1+\dots+b^{\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1})}\right\rceil\,.

(7.2)

As a consequence of this definition and (
7.1e
), we have that for all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}\leq k\leq q+\bar{n}
,

\displaystyle\Gamma_{q}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}\lambda_{q}^{2}\lambda_{k}^{4}\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-4}\lambda_{k}^{2}\lambda_{k-1}^{-4}<\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}\,,\quad\Gamma_{q}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-2000}\,,

(7.3a)

\displaystyle\Gamma_{q}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+500}\Lambda_{q}\left(\nicefrac{{\lambda_{k}}}{{\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}}}\right)^{2}\lambda_{k-1}^{-2}\lambda_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-200}\,.

(7.3b)

(iii)

Choose
\alpha=\alpha(q)\in(0,1)
such that

\displaystyle\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{\alpha}=\Gamma_{q}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}\,.

(7.4)

(iv)

Choose
\mathrm{T}_{q}
for lossy material derivative costs, according to the formula

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{T}_{q-1}^{-1}=\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+100}\delta_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}+\Gamma_{q}^{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}+100}\delta_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}\Lambda_{q}^{3}\,.

(7.5)

(v)

For
k\geq q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
, the intermittent pressure
\pi_{q}^{k}
becomes homogeneous in the sense that
\left\|\pi_{q}^{k}\right\|_{L^{p}}\sim\left\|\pi_{q}^{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}
. Such number
\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}
is chosen to satisfy

\displaystyle\Gamma_{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{4}\leq\Gamma_{q+\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{pr}}+1}\,.

(7.6)

(vi)

The number
\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t}
and
\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,x}

are auxiliary parameters used to define cutoff functions and pressure increments. It is large enough to absorb a ‘‘Sobolev loss", roughly speaking. More precisely, we choose them to satisfy

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t}

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,x}\,,

(7.7a)

\displaystyle\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{200}\left(\frac{\Gamma_{q-1}}{\Gamma_{q}}\right)^{\frac{\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t}}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq\min\left(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{-4}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{2},\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-17-{\mathsf{C}_{b}}}\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{2}r_{q}\right)\,,

(7.7b)

\displaystyle\delta_{q+\bar{n}}^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}r_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{\nicefrac{{\mathsf{C_{\infty}}}}{{2}}+16+{\mathsf{C}_{b}}}\left(\frac{\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}-1}}{\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}}\right)^{\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,x}}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q+\bar{n}}^{-1}\,.

(7.7c)

(vii)

Choose the number
\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}
of sharp material derivative estimates of velocity and errors propagated inductively
such that

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}\geq\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t},\quad\Gamma_{q}^{-\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}(\tau_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q}^{i+40})^{-\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t}-1}(\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1}\Gamma_{q})^{\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t}+1}\leq 1\,.

(7.8)

(viii)

The numbers of spatial/material derivative estimates of errors propagated inductively are described by
\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}
, chosen such that
23
23

23

The parameters
N_{g}
and
N_{c}
are used in the proof of the mollification results from Lemma
3.33
. For details we refer to
[
25
, Lemma 3.1, Proposition A.24]
.

\displaystyle N_{g}\leq N_{c}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}{40}\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}\,,

(7.9a)

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma_{q-1}^{\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}\Gamma_{q}^{-\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{q+5\bar{n}}^{3}\Gamma_{q}^{-2\mathsf{C_{\infty}}-3}r_{q}\,.

(7.9b)

(ix)

Choose
{\mathsf{N}_{\rm dec}}
such that

\displaystyle(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}+2}\Gamma_{q})^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\Gamma_{q}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{10}}}}{{4\pi}}\right)^{\mathsf{N}_{\rm dec}}\,,\qquad\qquad\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}\leq{\mathsf{N}_{\rm dec}}\,.

(7.10)

(x)

Choose
K_{\circ}
large enough so that

\lambda_{q}^{-K_{\circ}}\leq\delta_{q+3\bar{n}}^{3}\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{{5\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}}\lambda_{q+\bar{n}+2}^{-100}\,.

(7.11)

(xi)

Choose
{\mathsf{d}}
and
N_{**}
such that

\displaystyle 2{\mathsf{d}}+3

\displaystyle\leq N_{**}\,,

(7.12a)

\displaystyle\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{100}\Gamma_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{{\mathsf{d}}}}{{200}}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}+2}^{5+K_{\circ}}\left(1+\frac{\max(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{2}\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1},\Lambda_{q}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}})}{\tau_{q}^{-1}}\right)^{20\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{200\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\,,

(7.12b)

\displaystyle\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{100}\Gamma_{q}^{-\nicefrac{{N_{**}}}{{20}}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}+2}^{5+K_{\circ}}\left(1+\frac{\max(\lambda_{q+\bar{n}}^{2}\mathrm{T}_{q}^{-1},\Lambda_{q}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Lambda_{q+\bar{n}})}{\tau_{q}^{-1}}\right)^{20\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{T}_{q+\bar{n}}^{20\mathsf{N}_{\textnormal{ind,t}}}\,.

(7.12c)

(xii)

The number
\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}
quantifies the maximum number of spatial/material derivatives used throughout the scheme. We choose it so that

\displaystyle 2{\mathsf{N}_{\rm dec}}+4+10\mathsf{N}_{\rm ind}

\displaystyle\leq\nicefrac{{\mathsf{N}_{\rm fin}}}{{40000}}-{\mathsf{d}}^{2}-10\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,x}-10\mathsf{N}_{\rm cut,t}-N_{**}-300\,.

(7.13a)

(xiii)

Having chosen all the parameters mentioned in subsection
2.1
and items (
ii
)–(
xii
) except for
a
, there exists a sufficiently large parameter
a_{*}
such that
a_{*}^{(b-1)\varepsilon_{\Gamma}b^{-2\bar{n}}}
is at least fives times larger than
all
the implicit constants throughout the paper, as well as those which have been suppressed in the computations in this section. Choose
a
to be any natural number larger than
a_{*}
.

7.2 A few more inequalities

As a consequence of parameter choices, a number of additional inequalities hold. For the full list, we refer to
[
25
, section 11.2]
. In this paper, we shall use that all
q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-1\leq m\leq m^{\prime}\leq{q+\bar{n}}
,

\displaystyle\Gamma_{q}^{500+5{\mathsf{C}_{b}}}\lambda_{q}\left(\frac{\delta_{q+\bar{n}}}{\delta_{m+\bar{n}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\Lambda_{q}^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\left(\lambda_{m^{\prime}-1}^{-2}\lambda_{m^{\prime}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\left(\frac{\min(\lambda_{m},\lambda_{q+\bar{n}})\Gamma_{q}}{\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\lambda_{m-1}^{-2}\lambda_{m}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{q}^{-250}\,,

(7.14a)

\displaystyle\lambda_{q}\Gamma_{q}^{250}\Lambda_{q}^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\left(\frac{r_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}}{r_{q}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}\Gamma_{q}}{\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\lambda_{q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}}^{-1}\delta_{q+\bar{n}}^{{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}_{q+\bar{n}+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}+1}\,,

(7.14b)

\displaystyle\delta_{q+\bar{n}}\Gamma_{q}^{500}\Lambda_{q}^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\left(\lambda_{m-1}^{2}\lambda_{m}^{-1}\right)^{-\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\leq\delta_{m+\bar{n}}\quad\textnormal{for}\quad q+\nicefrac{{\bar{n}}}{{2}}-5\leq m

\displaystyle\leq q+\bar{n}+5\,.

(7.14c)

Appendix A
Appendix

In this section, we collect a number of useful tools from
[
6
]
and
[
